Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the chemistry framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 868. The resulting operator achieves bound O(906/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 868 + O(1/n^906). Summing over all modes and applying the chemistry convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
